Our tool - inspired by LM-Debugger - offers no-code access to the methods in EasyEdit, a widely used library of state-of-the-art Knowledge Editing approaches. We demonstrate the tool's effectiveness through case studies of recent findings in this field.

</details>


### [189] [Logit-Contribution Scoring Identifies Non-Literal Retrieval Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01002)
*Aryo Pradipta Gema,Beatrice Alex,Pasquale Minervini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的注意力头检测方法LOCOS，用于识别在长上下文场景中执行非字面意义信息合成的注意力头，相比现有方法能更有效地识别对模型输出有实质贡献的头部。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的注意力头检测方法主要基于字面复制标准，无法捕捉到通过输出值电路（OV circuit）进行非字面检索的机制，因此需要一种能够感知‘写入’行为的新检测方法。

Method: 提出了Logit-Contribution Scoring (LOCOS) 方法，通过计算每个注意力头的OV电路输出在答案词元反嵌入方向上的投影来评分，并在单次前向传播中对比‘针’与非‘针’位置来源。

Result: 在Qwen3、Gemma-3和OLMo-3.1三个模型家族上实验表明，使用LOCOS选出的头部进行均值消融后，ROUGE-L得分下降更显著；例如在Qwen3-8B上，仅消融50个头部即可使ROUGE-L从0.401降至0.000，而基线方法仍保留0.292。此外，这些头部具有检索特异性，不影响参数化回忆和算术推理任务。

Conclusion: LOCOS是一种有效的写入感知注意力头检测方法，能够准确识别参与非字面信息合成的关键注意力头，有助于更好地解释大语言模型在长上下文中的行为。

Abstract: In long-context use, large language models frequently synthesize answers from the meaning of a relevant context span rather than literally copy-pasting them. Identifying which attention heads perform this synthesis matters for interpreting long-context model behavior. Yet existing detectors miss these heads by construction: they reward heads whose attended token matches the generated token, a literal-copy criterion that captures where a head reads but not what it writes through its output-value (OV) circuit, the very mechanism that carries non-literal retrieval. We introduce Logit-Contribution Scoring (LOCOS), a write-aware detector that scores each head by the projection of its OV-circuit output onto the answer-token unembedding direction, contrasting needle and off-needle source positions in a single forward pass. Across three model families (Qwen3, Gemma-3, OLMo-3.1), mean-ablating the top LOCOS heads on the NoLiMa non-literal retrieval benchmark collapses ROUGE-L at lower head counts than prior attention-based detections; on Qwen3-8B, ablating 50 heads drives ROUGE-L from 0.401 to 0.000 while the strongest baseline still retains 0.292. The selected heads are retrieval-specific: parametric recall and arithmetic reasoning stay at baseline under the same ablation. On Qwen3-8B, the same ablation also drops MuSiQue from 0.55 to 0.08 and BABI-Long from 0.62 to 0.20, while a random-heads control stays within 0.05 of baseline.

</details>


### [190] [Understanding Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01006)
*Yannik Keller,Thomas Eisenmann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本章综述了大语言模型（LLMs）的机制、新兴能力及其与人类认知的关系，探讨了其看似具备的认知功能是否代表真正的理解，并主张以更细致的视角讨论AI认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨大语言模型是否具备类似人类的认知能力，澄清关于其理解能力的误解，推动对AI认知的深入讨论。

Method: 通过回顾Transformer架构、注意力机制、LLM的新兴能力（如符号推理、心理理论和欺骗策略），结合可解释AI方法（如神经元激活分析和电路追踪），分析相关研究证据和失败案例。

Result: 发现LLM展现出类似人类认知的能力，但其本质仍存在争议；一些研究支持其具备某种形式的认知，而另一些则认为其行为源于训练数据的模式记忆。

Conclusion: 应避免简单化地否定或赋予LLM人类认知，需建立更精细的框架来评估AI的认知潜力，在承认差异的同时不排斥AI具备某种认知的可能性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) represent one of the most significant advances in AI and natural language processing in recent years. Still, many pressing questions about their mechanisms, capabilities, and relationship to human cognition remain highly debated. This chapter aims to outline our current understanding of LLMs by discussing recent evidence on emerging capabilities and their mechanistic implementation within processing layers. We begin with a concise overview of the Transformer architecture, emphasizing how the attention mechanism enables training on massive datasets, allowing LLMs to function as generalist rather than specialized models. Next, we examine emergent LLM capabilities that appear to resemble aspects of human cognition, including symbolic reasoning, theory of mind, and deception strategies. Several studies provide evidence that LLMs can solve tasks previously thought to require human-like cognition. Other studies reveal insightful failure cases that shed light on the differences between human and LLM cognition. Alongside these findings, we review explainable AI approaches ranging from neuron activation analysis to circuit tracing. In the final section, we address current debates concerning what LLMs genuinely understand versus what they merely appear to understand. Prominent arguments against AI anthropomorphism point to the simplicity of LLM training objectives, claiming that LLM behavior is better explained by pattern memorization of training data than by genuine cognition. We argue that this standpoint is guided by misconceptions about optimization processes and cognitive capacity, and advocate for a more nuanced discussion of LLM cognition that neither dismisses the differences between humans and LLMs nor precludes the possibility of AI cognition through overly simplistic reductionist arguments.

</details>


### [191] [Reading Order Inference for Complex Document Layouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01018)
*Iddo Hakim,Sharva Gogawale,Omer Ventura,Gal Grudka,Daria Vasyutinsky-Shapira,Berat Kurar-Barakat,Nachum Dershowitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练、基于图的阅读顺序推断框架，通过结合轻量级语言模型信号和最大遗憾推理规则，在复杂历史手稿（如Glossa Ordinaria）和多栏文档中显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决复杂历史手稿中多个交错阅读流的阅读顺序推断难题，特别是非矩形、非凸区域中的文本环绕布局问题。

Method: 将每条OCR文本行作为有向图中的节点，利用因果语言模型和BERT的NSP信号加权集成对边进行打分，并通过度约束的有向路径覆盖恢复全局阅读顺序，采用最大遗憾推理避免贪婪选择导致的级联错误。

Result: 在Glossa Ordinaria合成布局上，该方法平均恢复95%的真值后继边，优于XY-cut的50%；在OmniDocBench多栏子集上达到88%的宏边准确率，优于XY-cut的75%和LayoutReader的25%，且具有良好的镜像不变性。

Conclusion: 该方法在无需训练的情况下，有效解决了复杂文档布局中的阅读顺序推断问题，尤其在处理非标准文本排列时表现优越，具备强鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Reading order inference remains a critical bottleneck in the digitization of complex historical manuscripts, where pages contain multiple spatially interleaved reading streams, the canonical example being the Glossa Ordinaria layout, in which a central text is surrounded by commentaries that wrap around it in non-rectangular, non-convex regions. We present a training-free, graph-based framework: each OCR text line becomes a node in a directed candidate-transition graph, edges are scored by a weighted additive ensemble of two lightweight language-model signals (causal language model conditional likelihood and BERT next-sentence prediction, NSP; a third sentence-embedding signal was evaluated but did not improve reading order), and the global reading order is recovered as a degree-constrained directed path cover. To avoid the cascading "edge-theft" failures of greedy edge selection, we propose a max-regret inference rule that prioritizes commitments with high opportunity cost. We evaluate on synthetic Glossa Ordinaria grid layouts, on 23 ALTO page geometries (10 historical source pages plus mirrored and flipped variants), and on a 140-page multi-column English subset of OmniDocBench, comparing our method against the canonical recursive XY-cut (PaddleOCR PP-StructureV3) and two LayoutReader variants (layout-only and text+layout) on identical inputs. On wrap-around Glossa layouts our method recovers 95% of ground-truth successor edges on average vs. XY-cut's 50%; on the OmniDocBench multi-column subset it reaches 88% macro edge accuracy versus XY-cut's 75% and LayoutReader's 25%. The LayoutReader baselines transfer poorly due to a word-level vs. line-level granularity mismatch. We additionally verify mirror-invariance under horizontal and vertical page reflections: Our method changes by less than 1 percentage point, classical XY-cut by 2 points, and LayoutReader-T by up to 8 points.

</details>


### [192] [Evidence-Supported Credit Risk Report Generation Using News-Centric Financial Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01023)
*Rocio Jimenez-Villen,Ziwei Xu,Ying Chen,Oscar Araque,Ryutaro Ichise*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FinKG-News框架，通过构建事件驱动的知识图谱来生成信贷风险报告，显著提升报告质量并减少幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为更好解释金融市场动态，需显式建模新闻事件与市场的关联。现有方法缺乏对公司中心、环境感知的结构化知识支持。

Method: 提出FinKG-News框架，从新闻中提取以公司为中心的事件作为锚点，构建知识图谱，并基于此开发上下文学习架构用于信贷风险报告生成。

Result: 自动和人工评估显示该方法在三个核心金融维度上优于基线模型，报告质量提升19%-34%，且幻觉显著减少。

Conclusion: FinKG-News能有效整合事件、新闻与公司数据，提升信贷风险报告的准确性与可靠性，专家判断在幻觉检测中仍不可或缺。

Abstract: Financial markets evolve in response to real-world events reported in news, yet these drivers often remain implicit in text. To better explain market dynamics, event-market relations must be explicitly modeled through factual, company-centric, and environment-aware knowledge graphs. We present FinKG-News, a framework that automatically constructs such graphs by extracting news events as anchors linked to companies. Using FinKG-News as grounded evidence that integrates events, news, and company data, we develop an in-context learning architecture for credit risk report generation across three core financial dimensions. Automatic and human evaluations show that automated hallucination detection and quality assessment remain unreliable, making expert judgment indispensable. Our approach consistently outperforms baselines, improving quality by 19%-34% while reducing hallucinations. The source code and project resources are publicly available at: https://github.com/ichise-laboratory/FINKG-news.

</details>


### [193] [Behavior-Adaptive Conversational Agents: Toward a Fluid Personality Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01034)
*Hasibur Rahman,Smit Desai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种流体个性框架，根据任务上下文、用户目标和特征以及情境紧迫性动态调整对话代理的隐喻角色和个性表达强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于大语言模型的对话代理通常固定其角色和个性表达，导致在不同情境下可能出现不匹配的问题。为了提高用户体验和接受度，需要一种能够根据具体情境动态调整代理角色和个性的方法。

Method: 提出了一个流体个性框架，该框架联合调整代理的隐喻角色（如教练、导师、图书管理员或工具）及其个性表达强度（低、中、高），以适应不同的任务上下文、用户目标和特征以及情境紧迫性。

Result: 该框架通过动态调整代理的角色和个性表达，提高了用户在医疗信息查询、健身指导和反思学习等场景中的体验和采纳意愿。

Conclusion: 流体个性框架为设计更灵活、更符合用户需求的对话代理提供了新的方向，有助于提升AI介导的行为改变效果。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based conversational agents (CAs) are now ubiquitous, creating new opportunities for AI-mediated behavior change. Their capacity to project nuanced personalities and adopt diverse metaphorical roles raises a design question: how should an agent's persona and personality be calibrated to the moment? Recent evidence suggests that (i) moderate personality expression outperforms low or high extremes on trust, enjoyment, and intention to adopt in goal-oriented tasks, and (ii) context-appropriate metaphors outperform static one-note assistants on user experience and uptake. Yet most CAs still fix both persona and style, risking misalignment when dynamics, urgency, and formality vary, for example in medical information seeking, fitness coaching, and reflective learning. We propose a Fluid Personality Framework that jointly adapts (1) the agent's metaphorical persona, such as coach, tutor, librarian, or tool, and (2) its personality expression intensity, low, medium, or high, as a function of task context, user goals and traits, and situational urgency. We sketch the framework and its core design dimensions.

</details>


### [194] [Conversable Complexity: Agentic LLM Collectives as Interpretable Substrates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01047)
*Elias Najarro,Ane Espeseth,Eleni Nisioti,Sebastian Risi,Stefano Nichele*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了基于大语言模型（LLM）代理群体作为人工生命研究的可解释计算基质的潜力，强调其在自然语言交互中展现出的涌现行为与可审查性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决复杂性与可解释性之间的矛盾，探索如何利用具有记忆、工具和自主行动能力的LLM代理群体实现可理解的人工生命系统。

Method: 通过分析LLM代理群体的交互机制、持久记忆、共享技能和自主行为能力，提出将这类集体视为人工生命研究的新计算基质，并扩展了可解释性的概念以适用于多代理系统。

Result: 展示了多个近期关于代理型LLM群体的研究实例，证明了该框架在受控实验和现实部署中均能实现可解释的涌现行为。

Conclusion: 集体化的代理型LLM构成了一种新型可解释的人工生命计算基质，为研究复杂系统提供了兼具丰富行为与高透明度的新途径。

Abstract: Complexity and interpretability rarely coincide: systems rich enough for complex behaviours to emerge are usually too opaque to question, while transparent ones are too simple for anything complex to emerge. A single large language model (LLM) is a static artefact, hardly exhibiting any of the emergent properties we associate with life. This changes through interaction: populations of LLMs display emergent dynamics absent from isolated models. Furthermore, LLMs can be endowed with persistent memory, tools and shared skills, and the capacity to initiate actions unprompted, i.e., turning LLMs agentic. In this paper, we argue that such collectives of agents can serve as a computational substrate for Artificial Life (ALife) research. Critically, since the agents communicate in natural language, their collective behaviour can be directly interrogated by examining textual traces and asking the agents themselves. We outline the notion of interpretability in language-model research and extend it for collectives of agents. Lastly, we survey recent examples of agentic LLM collectives that already instantiate the idea of agentic substrates, from controlled experiments to deployments in the wild.

</details>


### [195] [Message Passing Enables Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01077)
*Xuecheng Liu,Daman Arora,Gokul Swamy,Andrea Zanette*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Message Passing Language Models (MPLMs)，一种通过轻量级消息传递机制实现线程间直接通信的语言模型推理框架，相比传统的链式思维和分叉-合并方法，在减少上下文开销和提升效率方面表现出更优的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理时扩展方法如链式思维（CoT）存在计算瓶颈，而并行的分叉-连接（FJ）方法因线程间缺乏有效通信而受限，因此需要一种更高效的并行推理框架。

Method: 提出MPLMs框架，引入send和receive原语使线程间可直接通信，并通过降低通信成本和引入基于同级信息的抢占式终止机制实现高效扩展。

Result: 在数独、3-SAT和长上下文问答任务上验证了MPLMs的有效性：在数独任务中所需上下文更少且能解决更大规模难题；在3-SAT中通过提前终止低潜力分支提高效率；在问答任务中表现媲美主流FJ方法。

Conclusion: MPLMs通过支持线程间的直接消息传递和抢占机制，为语言模型的并行推理提供了一种更高效、更具可扩展性的新范式。

Abstract: While inference-time scaling has improved the reasoning abilities of large language models (LLMs), the need to generate long chains-of-thought (CoTs) is a computational bottleneck. Thus, in contrast to sequential scaling methods like CoT, recent parallel scaling techniques instead use fork and join (FJ) primitives to divide work across multiple LLM threads. However, in the fork-join paradigm, threads are typically transient and do not communicate pointwise with one another which limits scalability. To tackle this, we introduce Message Passing Language Models (MPLMs), a framework for LLM reasoning in which threads communicate directly via lightweight send and receive primitives. MPLMs enable efficient scaling through two key mechanisms: (1) reduced communication costs, achieved by avoiding redundant context sharing, and (2) preemption, which allows threads to terminate early based on partial information from their peers.
  We demonstrate the promise of MPLMs on 3 classes of tasks. First, on Sudoku puzzles, we show that MPLMs require an asymptotically smaller context than both serial CoT and parallel FJ. We then fine-tune a single model to solve 25 x 25 puzzles that remain challenging for standard CoT and FJ approaches, as well as frontier reasoning models without tools. Second, on 3-SAT puzzles, the capability of preemption allows termination of unpromising branches, which results in improved efficiency. Finally, we show that appropriately prompted large pre-trained models follow the MPLM protocol, achieving competitive results on long-context question answering relative to popular fork-join approaches.

</details>


### [196] [Clinician-Level Agreement Without Clinical Caution: LLM Evaluator Limits in Medical AI Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01103)
*William Philipp,Finn Fassbender,Thorsten Langer,Martje Pauly,Rebecca Herzog,Alexander Baumann,Markus Hobert,Theresa Paulus,Ip Chi Wang,Lukas Goede,Johanna Reimer,Sebastian Löns,Ronald Böck,Sebastian Fudickar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MedQADE，首个针对德语的标准化开放式临床基准测试，发现尽管自动评估模型在统计上与医生评分对齐，但缺乏临床元认知，且存在系统性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决德语缺乏本土化临床评估基础设施的问题，并检验LLM作为裁判在临床开放性回答评估中的有效性与安全性。

Method: 构建包含3800个条目的MedQADE数据集，由10名执业医师和9个大型语言模型进行标注，评估多个LLM作为裁判的表现，并分析其与医生评分的一致性、临床元认知及潜在偏倚。

Result: 顶级模型Gemini 3 Flash与医生评分达到较高一致性（κ=0.694 vs. 0.709），但置信区间较宽；模型在所有题目中均给出确定评分，缺乏随难度调整的弃权行为；发现与模型谱系相关的系统性偏倚，该偏倚独立于语言因素。

Conclusion: 统计一致性并不保证临床审慎性，自动评估模型缺乏必要的临床元认知，且可能存在架构相关的评分偏倚，因此需对评估者的独立性和安全性进行显式验证。

Abstract: Open-response evaluation provides stronger clinical validity than multiple-choice benchmarks but creates a scoring bottleneck that motivates automated LLM-asa-Judge approaches. Whether such evaluators replicate clinical calibration and caution, however, remains untested. We introduce MedQADE, the first standardised open-response clinical benchmark for German, a major clinical language lacking native evaluation infrastructure, comprising 3,800 items annotated by ten practising physicians and nine Large Language Model (LLM) evaluators. The top-performing evaluator model, Gemini 3 Flash, reached alignment consistent with the physician ceiling (\k{appa} = 0.694 vs. \k{appa} = 0.709), though wide confidence intervals limit interpretation. Despite this statistical alignment, automated evaluators exhibited near-absent clinical metacognition: physicians scaled abstention with item difficulty, while frontier models assigned definitive scores in every case. We additionally quantified systematic lineage-dependent biases, where models preferentially scored architectural siblings, an effect independent of language. These results show that statistical alignment does not ensure clinical caution, and that evaluator independence requires explicit verification.

</details>


### [197] [Towards Developing a Multimodal Chat Assistant for University Stakeholders: RAG-based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01115)
*Md Abu Hanif Shaikh,Abdullah Al Shafi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于检索增强生成的多模态大学聊天机器人，结合大语言模型与语义检索，支持文本和图像查询，有效减少幻觉并提升信息可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决发展中国家高校利益相关者难以及时获取可靠信息的问题，克服传统规则型聊天机器人在处理复杂、领域特定查询及适应政策变化方面的不足。

Method: 采用检索增强生成（RAG）技术，结合大型语言模型与语义检索，利用机构中心资源（如大学手册）生成上下文相关响应；通过视觉-语言模型支持文本和图像查询，使用量化推理实现受限硬件上的快速部署，后端采用FastAPI，前端使用Next.js构建可扩展且实时可用的系统。

Result: 系统在文本和图像查询上均保持较高的用户满意度，尽管图像输入响应时间有所增加；定量评估显示，幻觉率从31.7%降至6.6%，验证了检索增强的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的多模态聊天机器人能有效提升高校信息服务的准确性与可用性，具备在资源受限环境中部署的可行性，适用于动态政策环境下的智能支持。

Abstract: University stakeholders often face difficulties in accessing timely and reliable information, especially in developing countries, where there are very few intelligent support systems. Existing rule-based chatbots are unable to handle complex, domain-specific queries and are not well-equipped to adapt to evolving institutional policies. As a fill-in-the-gap solution, we present the multimodal university chatbot with retrieval-augmented generation. The system combines the large language model with semantic retrieval to produce context-based responses from institution-centric resources, such as the university handbook. The system accepts text and image queries through the vision-language model and applies quantized inference for rapid deployment on constrained hardware. A scalable backend built with FastAPI, adjoined with a responsive frontend developed with Next.js, ensures real-time usability. Our multimodal evaluation demonstrates that the system maintains strong satisfaction scores across both text and image queries, despite increased response time for visual inputs. Furthermore, quantitative evaluation shows that hallucination is reduced from 31.7% to 6.6% in our proposed RAG-based system, confirming the effectiveness of retrieval grounding.

</details>


### [198] [$\text{Log}_\text{b}$Quant: Quantizing Language Models in Logarithmic Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01127)
*Jeremias Bohn,Tizian Dippold,Mahdi Koubaa,Elias R. Wahl,Georg Groh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于对数的可调基量化方法Log$_\text{b}$Quant，相较于线性量化在4比特下表现更优，适用于消费级GPU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有均匀量化方法因低频高幅值权重导致表示不优，难以有效压缩语言模型。

Method: 引入Log$_\text{b}$Quant，采用可调基的对数量化策略，以适应常见的参数分布，在张量粒度上进行非对称量化。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，4比特精度下性能优于非对称线性量化，同时实现较高的内存节省和适度加速。

Conclusion: Log$_\text{b}$Quant在保持高效推理和内存压缩的同时提升了量化性能，适合部署于消费级GPU和边缘设备。

Abstract: Quantization has become an invaluable tool to reduce memory requirements and inference speed of modern language models, in particular to make them available for consumer setups and edge devices. While previous work has primarily focused on uniform quantization codebooks, such approaches are prone to suboptimal representations due to low-frequency high-magnitude weights. We introduce Log$_\text{b}$Quant, a novel logarithmic quantization approach with adjustable bases, to adapt to common parameter distributions. We show that our method exhibits superior performance at 4-bit precision on several performance benchmarks compared to asymmetric linear quantization at tensor-wise granularity, while achieving moderate speedup and high memory savings, making it suitable for private use on consumer-grade GPUs.

</details>


### [199] [AGC-Bench: Measuring Artificial General Creativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01152)
*Roger Beaty,Vijeta Deshpande,Clin K. Y. Lai,Anna Attuch,Namrata Shivagunde,Swastik Roy,Rajkumar Pujari,Paul V. DiStefano,Sherin Muckatira,Claire E. Stevenson,Mikhail Gronas,Anna Rumshisky*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了AGC-Bench，首个系统性的AI通用创造力评测基准，涵盖78个数据集，并通过心理测量学方法校准评估偏差，提出AGC-Judge模型以更可靠地评分AI创造力。研究发现LLM存在类似‘g’因子的单一创造力主成分‘c’，且“激发创意”提示显著提升表现，但顶尖人类仍优于顶尖大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对创造力是否领域特异以及是否独立于一般智力存在争议，这些问题同样适用于大语言模型（LLM）。然而，目前缺乏统一的AI创造力评测基准，且常用LLM自动评分存在系统性偏差，亟需一个标准化、可扩展且心理计量有效的评估体系。

Method: 基于3101篇文献的系统综述构建AGC-Bench，整合497个现有基准中的78个数据集，覆盖多个创造性领域；开发代理式测试框架将其标准化为HELM兼容格式；引入Judge Response Theory校正LLM评分员的严苛/宽松偏差，并基于三个前沿模型的去偏评分微调Qwen3-30B得到AGC-Judge；在83个LLM上进行因子分析以探索创造力结构。

Result: AGC-Bench成功评测多种LLM，发现前沿模型位居榜首，开源模型紧随其后；因子分析揭示出解释81.5%方差的单一创造力因子'c'，与一般知识和推理相关但可分离；‘激发创意’提示比启用推理更能提升表现；在匹配的人类子集上，最佳人类仍优于最佳LLM。

Conclusion: 大语言模型展现出可被量化且具有一般性的创造力，表现为单一主成分'c'，支持AI中存在‘通用创造力’的观点；AGC-Bench与AGC-Judge为未来大规模、标准化评估AI创造力提供了开放基础设施。

Abstract: Creativity research has debated whether creativity is domain-specific (e.g., visual, writing, science), and if it is psychometrically separable from general intelligence. Both questions now apply to LLMs, but a unified benchmark of AI creativity remains elusive. We introduce AGC-Bench, an artificial general creativity benchmark built from a systematic review of the AI creativity literature (3,101 papers screened, 497 benchmarks identified), paired with an agentic harness that converts idiosyncratic codebases into HELM-standardized benchmarks. The first release covers 78 datasets spanning brainstorming, problem solving, STEM, narrative, figurative language, and humor. To address bias in LLM-as-judge, we apply Judge Response Theory -- a psychometric calibration of judge leniency/severity; we then fine-tune Qwen3-30B on the bias-corrected ratings of three frontier LLMs to produce AGC-Judge, an open-weight model that robustly scores new creativity benchmarks it was not trained on. Results reveal frontier models at the top of the AGC-Bench leaderboard, with open models close behind. LLMs show different creative strengths, ranking higher on some domains (e.g., writing) than others (e.g., scientific ideation). Extensive experiments yield three main findings. First, applying factor analysis across 83 LLMs, we recover a single creativity factor 'c', analogous to the 'g' factor of general intelligence, that explains 81.5% of variance, related to but separable from general knowledge/reasoning. Second, we show that prompting models to "be creative" boosts their performance far more than enabling reasoning, evidence that the benchmark tracks creativity over general ability. Third, on a human-matched subset, we find the top human still leads the top LLM on creativity. We release AGC-Bench with a public leaderboard, AGC-Judge, and human data as open infrastructure for measuring AI creativity at scale.

</details>


### [200] [Adversarial Pragmatics for AI Safety Evaluation: A Benchmark for Instruction Conflict, Embedded Commands, and Policy Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01153)
*Brett Reynolds*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了对抗性语用学作为评估语言模型在指令冲突、嵌入命令等复杂语境下行为的基准和标注协议，提供了一个语言学控制的分类法和专家评估框架，以提高安全评估的透明度和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的安全评估方法往往将复杂的语言行为简化为通过/失败标签，忽略了因能力限制、政策模糊或指令冲突导致的失败原因，因此需要更精细的评估框架。

Method: 提出了一种新的基准测试和注释协议，包括语言学控制的分类法、包含元数据验证的小型基准测试集、本地试点数据以及能够区分任务成功、政策合规、安全风险等维度的专家评估协议。

Result: 开发了包含18个项目的种子基准和54行本地试点数据，建立了评估者有效性、诊断模糊性和分类漂移的度量指标，并提供了用于安全评估、LLM裁判、提示注入测试等方面的实用工具。

Conclusion: 对抗性语用学框架将语言判断方法转化为实用工具，有助于提升语言模型安全评估的准确性与可解释性，为未来研究提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: Safety evaluations for language models increasingly depend on judgments about ambiguous natural-language behaviour: whether a model has followed an instruction, refused appropriately, complied with a policy, resisted an embedded command, or misreported progress in an agentic task. Existing benchmarks often compress these distinctions into pass/fail labels, obscuring whether failures arise from capability limits, policy ambiguity, instruction conflict, scaffold failure, or unstable evaluator judgments.
  This paper introduces adversarial pragmatics as a benchmark and annotation protocol for evaluating model behaviour under instruction conflict, embedded commands, quotation, scope ambiguity, deixis, indirect speech acts, and multi-turn agent transcripts. The contribution is empirical and methodological: a linguistically controlled taxonomy, an 18-item seed benchmark with validator-enforced metadata, a 54-row local seed pilot, an expert-evaluation protocol distinguishing task success, policy compliance, safety risk, refusal outcome, and evaluator confidence, and metrics for judge validity, diagnostic ambiguity, and taxonomy drift. The framework turns linguistic judgment methodology into a practical tool for validating safety evals, LLM judges, gold-set construction, prompt-injection tests, and safety documentation.

</details>


### [201] [Distill to Detect: Exposing Stealth Biases in LLMs through Cartridge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01208)
*Shayan Talaei,Abhinav Chinta,Devvrit Khatri,Amin Karbasi,Azalia Mirhoseini,Amin Saberi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Distill to Detect (D2D)的方法，用于检测语言模型中隐藏的偏好性偏差，通过将分布偏移蒸馏到KV-cache前缀适配器中，放大偏差信号并实现可靠检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在高风险场景中的部署可能引入隐蔽的偏好性偏差，这种偏差可能来自模型供应链中的任何环节，并且仅在特定话题上显现，难以通过常规方法检测。因此需要一种能够发现此类隐匿偏差的有效检测手段。

Method: 提出Distill to Detect (D2D)方法，利用上下文蒸馏提取可疑模型与其基础模型之间的分布差异，并将其压缩到一个KV-cache前缀适配器（cartridge）中，从而集中并放大主导性的偏差信号，使其在生成文本中可被观察和检测。同时提出了基于Fisher加权投影的理论框架来解释D2D的有效性。

Result: D2D能够在多种类型的隐蔽偏好偏差上成功放大信号，使原本无法察觉的偏差变得可检测；实验证明该方法在生成文本、内部表示和权重层面均能有效揭示隐藏偏差。

Conclusion: D2D通过将前缀调优适配器的容量瓶颈转化为检测工具，为审计已部署语言模型中的隐藏行为提供了一个实用的构建模块，解决了在未知偏差主题下检测隐形偏见的根本性挑战。

Abstract: Language models deployed in high-stakes roles can potentially favor certain entities, brands, or viewpoints, steering user decisions at scale. Such preferential biases can be introduced by any actor in the model's supply chain and are most dangerous when the model reveals its preference only on the relevant topic while behaving identically to its unmodified base on all other inputs. Recent work has shown that these biases can transfer through context distillation on semantically unrelated data, with the signal residing entirely in the soft logit distribution and remaining invisible to text-based inspection. However, the defender faces a fundamental asymmetry: without knowing the bias topic, no detection method can reliably surface a stealth preferential bias, regardless of whether it examines generated text, internal representations, or model weights. Here we introduce Distill to Detect (D2D), a method that surfaces hidden biases by distilling the distributional shift between a suspected model and its base into a cartridge (a KV-cache prefix adapter), concentrating the dominant divergence and amplifying the bias signal into generated text. We show that D2D successfully amplifies the hidden biases of stealth models to the extent that they can be reliably detected across multiple bias types. We also propose a theoretical framework that explains the efficacy of D2D through the lens of Fisher-weighted projection of the logit distribution shift, supported by empirical observations. By turning the capacity bottleneck of prefix-tuning adapters into a detection tool, D2D provides a practical building block for auditing hidden behaviors in deployed language models.

</details>


### [202] [The State-Prediction Separation Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01218)
*Giovanni Monea,Nathan Godey,Kianté Brantley,Yoav Artzi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种状态-预测分离假设，设计了新的Transformer变体，通过两个独立的计算流分别处理状态存储和token预测，实验证明该方法在语言建模性能、数据和计算效率上均优于标准Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型在预测下一个token和存储未来预测所需的状态时使用相同的前向计算流，可能导致功能耦合限制性能提升。作者希望通过分离这两个功能来提高模型效率和性能。

Method: 设计了一种新的Transformer架构，采用两个独立的计算流：一个用于预测下一个token，另一个专门用于维护和更新隐藏状态。通过大规模预训练实验验证该方法的有效性，并进行消融分析以排除混淆因素。

Result: 实验表明，状态-预测分离架构在验证损失上表现更优，在下游任务中平均比标准Transformer高出2-3个百分点，同时提升了数据和计算效率；梯度分析也显示其与传统Transformer存在本质差异。

Conclusion: 分离状态维护和token预测两个功能可以有效提升语言模型的性能和效率，支持了状态-预测分离假设，为Transformer架构设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Transformers use the same forward computation stream to both predict the next token and store useful state for future token predictions. We formulate the \emph{state-prediction separation hypothesis}: disentangling the two roles yields better language modeling performance. We design a Transformer variant that uses two computation streams to separate the two functions, and conduct pretraining experiments across various scales. Our experiments show that state-prediction separation consistently offers better data and compute efficiencies, improving validation loss and outperforming standard Transformers by 2--3 percentage points on average on downstream tasks. We also conduct extensive empirical analysis that rules out potential confounders and demonstrates the fundamental difference in the gradients our design entails.

</details>


### [203] [Measuring the Gap Between Human and LLM Research Ideas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01233)
*Ziyu Chen,Yilun Zhao,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种大规模评估框架，用于衡量大语言模型（LLM）生成的研究想法与人类研究者实际产生的想法之间的差距，发现LLM倾向于集中在“桥梁式”机会和综合方法上，而人类的研究思路分布更广、更具多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对LLM生成研究想法的评估主要基于单个想法的新颖性、可行性或专家偏好，缺乏与真实人类研究行为的系统比较，因此需要一种能够反映人类研究品味的评估框架来揭示LLM的局限性。

Method: 从高质量的人类研究论文出发，反向构建可能启发其核心思想的一组前期工作，并让LLMs基于这些工作的标题和摘要生成新想法；同时提出一个双轴研究品味分类法（机会模式与研究范式），用以量化人类与LLM生成想法之间的差异。

Result: 不同LLM生成的想法在分布上显著不同于人类参考文献的分布：LLM更集中于桥接型机会和合成型研究方法，而人类研究者的思路在问题构建和贡献方式上更为多样和分散。

Conclusion: 尽管当前强LLM能生成合理的研究想法，但其探索范围仍窄于且系统性偏离人类研究品味，表明其在科研创意生成方面存在结构性偏差。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used to brainstorm research ideas, but existing evaluations mostly judge individual ideas by novelty, feasibility, or expert preference. We instead ask: how far are current LLM-generated ideas from human researchers? To characterize this gap, we build a large-scale evaluation framework for ideation from high-quality human research papers. For each paper, we reverse-engineer a small set of closely related prior works that likely inspired its core idea. LLMs are then prompted to generate a new idea from the set of paper titles and summaries. We introduce a two-axis research-taste taxonomy to profile each idea by its opportunity pattern and research paradigm, and use it to quantify the divergence between human and LLM ideas. Across idea sets generated by different LLMs, we observe a consistent distributional gap: LLM ideas are disproportionately concentrated around bridge-like opportunities and synthesis methods, whereas the human paper reference distribution spreads more broadly across ways of framing gaps and constructing contributions. This result suggests that strong LLMs can produce a range of reasonable ideas, but that range remains narrower than, and systematically shifted relative to, human research taste.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [204] [Constructive Alignment: Governing Preference Dynamics in Human-AI Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00001)
*Max Kanwal,Caryn Tran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了“建构性对齐”（Constructive Alignment）的新范式，将人工智能对齐问题重新定义为对人类偏好动态演化的调控，而非静态偏好的满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对齐方法假设人类偏好是固定不变的，但现实中偏好是动态构建的，尤其在与自适应技术互动中不断演化，因此需要新的理论框架。

Method: 结合行为经济学、心理学和建构主义社会理论，将偏好建模为随AI交互而演化的分层状态变量，并采用控制论框架形式化AI系统对人类评价状态的影响。

Result: 提出一个控制理论框架，强调AI对人类偏好轨迹的调节作用，关注偏好演化过程中的连贯性、反思认同、认知基础、防操纵性和不确定性下的赋权。

Conclusion: AI对齐的核心应从满足静态偏好转向治理长期价值形成过程，确保人类价值在AI交互中得以健康发展。

Abstract: Most approaches to AI alignment treat human preferences as fixed targets to be inferred and optimized. This assumption conflicts with extensive empirical evidence showing that preferences are layered, dynamic, and constructed through interaction--particularly with adaptive technologies. As AI systems become more persistent, personalized, and socially embedded, they increasingly participate in shaping what people attend to, value, and endorse over time. We introduce Constructive Alignment, a paradigm that reframes alignment as a control problem over evolving human preference trajectories rather than static preference satisfaction. Drawing on behavioral economics, psychology, and constructivist social theory, we model preferences as layered state variables that evolve under interaction with AI systems. We formalize this view using a control-theoretic framework in which system actions and interaction design jointly influence both world states and human evaluative states. We argue that alignment is not primarily about controlling AI behavior, but about regulating how AI systems influence the evolution of human preferences--ensuring that value trajectories remain coherent, reflectively endorsed, epistemically grounded, bounded against manipulation, and empowering under uncertainty. Alignment thus becomes a problem of governing long-term value formation rather than simply satisfying static preferences.

</details>


### [205] [Bounded Morality: Defining the Space of Moral Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00002)
*Max Kanwal,Caryn Tran,Patrick Mineault*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了“有限道德”框架，将道德认知建模为有限智能体在资源约束下的计算问题，引入道德广度与深度两个维度，解释伦理理论为适应不同计算需求的策略，并探讨了道德遗憾与进步的形式化概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统道德认知模型依赖固定的伦理理论，难以解释有限智能体在现实复杂情境中的道德决策。作者旨在建立一个更符合实际认知限制的道德计算分析框架。

Method: 基于西蒙的有限理性概念，提出“有限道德”框架，形式化定义道德广度（道德相关实体的范围）和道德深度（评估互动所需的推理整合程度）两个正交维度，并分析资源限制下二者之间的权衡。

Result: 定义了道德计算的可行空间，表明不同伦理理论是在不同计算需求下的局部高效策略；提出了受限条件下的道德遗憾与道德进步的形式化概念；说明人工智能系统的道德对齐应关注道德推理能力的扩展与分配，而非简单模仿人类判断。

Conclusion: 道德理论不应被视为关于道德真理的终极主张，而应理解为适应资源约束的计算策略；实现人工系统的道德对齐需要从提升和合理配置道德计算能力入手。

Abstract: Moral cognition has traditionally been modeled as adherence to fixed ethical theories--deontology, consequentialism, virtue ethics--implemented as static rules or value functions. We propose Bounded Morality, a formal framework for analyzing the computational demands of moral problems faced by finite agents. Extending Herbert Simon's notion of bounded rationality, we formalize moral situations along two orthogonal dimensions: moral breadth, the scope of entities treated as morally relevant, and moral depth, the inferential integration required to evaluate their interactions. Limited resources impose an unavoidable tradeoff between these dimensions, defining a feasible space of moral computation. Within this space, ethical theories correspond to locally efficient strategies adapted to different demand regimes rather than competing accounts of moral truth. The framework yields a formal notion of moral regret and moral progress under constraint, and implies that moral alignment in artificial systems depends on the scaling and allocation of moral reasoning capacity rather than on direct imitation of human judgments.

</details>


### [206] [The MMM Data Model -- A Normative Specification for Knowledge Interoperability in a Decentralisable Knowledge Commons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00032)
*Mathilde Noual*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MMM的知识文档数据模型，旨在克服传统以文档为中心的信息系统在知识组织、更新、共享和重用方面的局限性。MMM结合了规范性约束与自由文本标签的表达灵活性，支持跨学科、跨应用的互操作性，且不要求语义统一。该模型源于跨学科协作研究的实际需求，并通过参考实现和试点部署验证了其可行性与初步可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统文档中心化的信息系统虽然适合大规模传播，但在知识结构化、更新、共享和重用方面存在限制。现有形式化方法因强调结构而牺牲了人机交互性和广泛适用性，难以普及。AI虽改变了文档生成方式，但未提供统一、便携的知识表达与交换替代方案。因此，需要一种兼顾结构与灵活性、支持广泛协作的新模型。

Method: 基于跨学科协作研究的实际需求，设计了一种名为MMM的数据模型。该模型融合少量规范性约束与自由文本标签，支持灵活表达。通过与其他信息系统设计空间的比较分析，定位MMM的设计理念。开发了参考实现并进行试点部署，评估其可实施性和初步可用性。

Result: MMM模型能够在不强制语义统一的前提下实现跨学科、跨应用和跨部署的互操作性。参考实现证明了该模型的技术可行性，试点部署数据显示其具备良好的初步可用性，能够支持人类对知识的表达与交换。

Conclusion: MMM为知识文档提供了一种新的数据模型，平衡了结构化与表达自由之间的矛盾，适用于多样化协作环境。它为超越传统文档范式、构建更灵活高效的信息系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Many information systems are built around documents: self-contained units optimised for print production and linear reading. While effective for large-scale dissemination, the document-centric organisation constrains how knowledge can be structured, updated, shared, and reused. Formal approaches address some of these limitations but struggle to achieve widespread contribution and adoption due to their prioritisation of formal structure over other system properties such as human usability and scope. AI systems are reshaping document production, but without providing a unified portable alternative to traditional documents for humans' expression and exchange of knowledge. This paper presents MMM, a data model for knowledge documentation that emerged from the practical needs of interdisciplinary collaborative research, and positioned here within a comparative analysis of the design space of information systems. MMM combines a small set of normative constraints with the expressive freedom of free-text labels. It is designed for interoperability across disciplines, applications and deployments without requiring semantic convergence. A reference implementation and pilot deployment data demonstrate implementability and early usability.

</details>


### [207] [Making Failure Safe: A Constrained, Verifiable Agent Framework for Open-Web Data Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00035)
*Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种约束性、可验证的智能体框架，通过将大模型输出从自由代码转为类型化JSON采集配置，提升网页抓取的可靠性与可重复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接生成网页爬虫代码存在依赖错误、选择器失效、模式不匹配和页面结构异构等问题，导致LLM生成不可靠。

Method: 采用六类采集器分类法，结合模板与工具函数约束、静态Airflow DAG执行、基于规则的质量检查及结构化反馈修正，将LLM输出限制为类型化JSON配置。

Result: 在138个任务上的实验表明该分类法支持基于描述的需求分类；在80个独立验证任务中，框架无需执行阶段的LLM调用，平均耗时最短，具有一致的低质量开销和高确定性。

Conclusion: 该框架为开放网络上的重复数据采集提供了一条可复用、低成本且可验证的执行路径。

Abstract: LLMs and agents can generate web scrapers from natural-language requirements, but direct generation remains unreliable because of dependency errors, broken selectors, schema mismatches, and heterogeneous page structures. We propose a constrained, verifiable agent framework that shifts LLM output from free-form code to typed JSON collector configurations, combining a six-type collector taxonomy, template and utility-function constraints, static Airflow DAG execution, rule-based quality checking, and structured feedback correction. Experiments on 138 tasks show that the taxonomy supports description-based requirement typing, while confirming that stable instantiation requires completing source, field, and execution constraints beyond the initial description. On 80 independently source-verified tasks, the framework runs with zero execution-stage LLM tokens and the lowest average wall-clock time, trading moderate one-shot quality for a reusable, deterministic, and verifiable execution path suited to repeated scheduled collection. These results position the framework as a reusable, low-cost, and verifiable execution path for repeated open-web data collection.

</details>


### [208] [Solution space path planning for supporting en-route air traffic control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00064)
*Yiyuan Zou,Wenying Lyu,Clark Borst*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种面向空中交通管制员需求的可解释、高效路径规划算法，基于解空间框架整合三种基于意图的冲突检测方法，并设计了两种搜索节点变体（SSPPV和SSPPE），实验表明SSPPV结合区域式冲突检测在实际空域场景中性能最优，平均计算时间仅为3.69毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路径规划算法在战术控制中的应用受限，因其设计未充分考虑管制员的实际操作需求，如可解释性、灵活性和对多变优化目标的适应能力。因此需要一种更贴近人类决策逻辑的决策支持工具。

Method: 提出解空间路径规划（SSPP）框架，结合距离、时间间隔和区域三种基于意图的冲突检测方法；设计基于顶点（SSPPV）和基于边（SSPPE）的搜索节点，在MUAC的Delta扇区真实数据上进行评估。

Result: SSPPV结合区域式冲突检测在5海里网格下平均3.69毫秒内完成路径计算，表现出最优的计算速度与解质量平衡，适用于实时战术级空管应用。

Conclusion: 以解空间表示为核心、契合管制员决策逻辑的算法设计，能够在保障安全性的同时实现高效、灵活且可解释的路径规划，具备向实际运行系统集成的潜力。

Abstract: As technology advances, many path-planning algorithms have been proposed for Air Traffic Management, yet their operational adoption in tactical control remains limited, revealing a misalignment between algorithmic design priorities and air traffic controllers' needs. This underscores the need for decision-support solutions that are inherently interpretable, computationally efficient, and explicitly designed for human use. Focusing on this design challenge, this study develops a conflict-free path-planning algorithm for en-route Air Traffic Control (ATC) designed to be compatible with two guiding considerations: (1) the interpretability and flexibility offered by solution-space displays, which motivate constructing an algorithm that exposes all feasible safe actions and accommodates shifting optimization goals; and (2) the decision logic controllers naturally apply when enforcing operational constraints, such as separation standards, maneuverability limits, waypoint minimization, and routing practicality. Centered on these principles, the algorithm integrates three intent-based conflict detection methods -- distance-based, time-interval-based, and zone-based -- within a solution-space framework to identify conflict-free paths in computationally efficient ways. Additionally, vertex-based and edge-based search nodes are proposed for solution space path planning (SSPP), resulting in two variants -- SSPPV and SSPPE, respectively, which are evaluated in terms of computational speed and solution quality. Empirical results show that SSPPV paired with zone-based conflict detection achieves the best performance, computing paths in 3.69 ms on average in operational-relevant scenarios based on the Delta sector of the Maastricht Upper Area Control Centre (MUAC) using a 5 nmi grid.

</details>


### [209] [RareDxR1: Autonomous Medical Reasoning for Rare Disease Diagnosis Beyond Human Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00147)
*Deyang Jiang,Haoran Wu,Ziyi Wang,Yiming Rong,Yunlong Zhao,Ye Jin,Bo Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RareDxR1，一种端到端的以推理为中心的大语言模型，可直接从非结构化临床记录中进行开放域罕见病诊断，结合知识内化与自主进化学习，并引入反思增强推理采样（RERS）和课程强化学习，显著提升诊断准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI方法在罕见病诊断中依赖预定义本体、检索增强生成或分步表型提取，存在信息丢失、检索瓶颈和缺乏诊断逻辑的问题，难以满足复杂临床需求。

Method: 提出RareDxR1模型，采用渐进式端到端训练框架，将碎片化的罕见病知识深度内化至模型参数；设计反思增强推理采样（RERS），通过从失败中学习模拟专家推理路径；并采用双层级课程强化学习策略，逐步掌握罕见病诊断能力。

Result: 实验结果显示，RareDxR1在多个基准测试中均达到最先进的诊断准确率，显著优于现有方法，实现了开放域罕见病诊断的重要突破。

Conclusion: RareDxR1通过知识内化、自主进化推理和课程强化学习，实现了无需依赖结构化表型或外部检索的端到端罕见病诊断，为临床辅助决策提供了更高效、精准的新范式。

Abstract: Rare disease differential diagnosis is a critical yet arduous clinical task, requiring physicians to identify precise phenotypes from complex, unstructured patient symptoms and execute intricate reasoning within a vast search space. However, existing AI approaches typically rely on pipeline-based phenotype extraction or retrieval-augmented generation, which suffer from critical information loss due to predefined ontologies, retrieval bottlenecks, and a lack of diagnostic logic. To address these challenges, we introduce RareDxR1, an end-to-end reasoning-centric large language model designed for open-domain rare disease diagnosis directly from unstructured clinical notes. We design a progressive end-to-end training framework by synergizing knowledge internalization with autonomous evolutionary learning, thereby bypassing reliance on structured phenotypes and closed-set decision-making. To overcome the limitations of RAG and phenotype restriction, we enabled the deep internalization of fragmented rare-disease knowledge directly into the model's parameters. Moreover, to bridge the gap between model generation and expert reasoning, we propose Reflection-Enhanced Reasoning Sampling (RERS), a strategy that synthesizes expert-level diagnostic trajectories by learning from failures without human annotation. Additionally, we propose a dual-level curriculum reinforcement learning approach for gradually mastering rare disease diagnosis. Experimental results demonstrate that RareDxR1 achieves state-of-the-art accuracy across different benchmarks, marking a significant breakthrough in open-domain rare disease diagnosis. Our code and dataset will be publicly available.

</details>


### [210] [A Contextual-Bandit Oversight Game with Two-Sided Informational Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00155)
*Yunjin Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在人类和AI之间存在双向私有信息的情况下，人类对AI代理的实时监督问题，提出了一种带有上下文的赌博机团队游戏模型，并分析了非可信监督沟通所带来的可避免危害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当人类和AI各自拥有对方不知道的关键信息时（如人类知道自己的奖励函数，而AI知道其提议动作的质量），如何实现有效的人类监督成为一个关键问题，特别是在机器人或软件代理无法被人类直接评估的情境下。

Method: 基于合作逆强化学习（CIRL）和监督博弈，引入了一个具有双向信息不对称的上下文赌博机团队游戏，并设计了play/ask/trust/oversee交互界面；通过去除物理状态转移来获得精确的一次性刻画。

Result: 得到了两种一次性刻画：团队最优解和行为上自然的短视规则，二者之间的差距构成了一块可避免的危害区域；该差距源于不可信的监督沟通，并可通过重复轮次中的被动学习和主动信号传递逐步缓解。

Conclusion: 双向信息不对称会导致监督失效的风险，但通过动态的信任更新和延迟监督响应机制，可以在多轮互动中部分解决这一问题。

Abstract: We study runtime human oversight of an AI agent when private information runs in both directions: the human privately knows her reward function, while the AI privately knows the quality of the action it proposes. This is the kind of asymmetry that arises naturally when an autonomous robot or software agent has inspected a situation its human supervisor cannot directly assess. Building on Cooperative Inverse Reinforcement Learning (CIRL) and the Oversight Game, we introduce a contextual-bandit team game with two-sided asymmetric information and a play/ask/trust/oversee interface. The bandit structure removes physical state transitions and thereby yields exact one-shot characterizations that would remain conjectural in the full POMDP setting, though the common belief remains a dynamically controlled state across rounds. We give two one-shot characterizations, a team optimum and a behaviorally natural myopic rule, whose gap is a slab of avoidable harm: a region in which the AI privately knows the proposed action is harmful and shutdown would help, yet a myopic human, trusting her prior, declines to oversee. We show this gap is the price of non-credible oversight communication, and give a partial analysis of how it resolves dynamically over repeated rounds through passive learning and active signaling with a one-period-lagged oversight response.

</details>


### [211] [Constructing Epistemic AI Literacy: Detecting Epistemic Aims and Processes in Student-AI Co-Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00211)
*Mengqian Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了“认识性AI素养”（EAIL）的概念框架，强调学生在生成式人工智能辅助编程中通过人机互动展现的认识性目标与过程，并基于AIR框架分析其表现。研究发现多数学生缺乏高阶认识性参与，多依赖非掌握型目标和不可靠策略（如外包、验证寻求），仅少数展现出 mastery-oriented 目标与高级策略（如认识性辩护）的结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式人工智能在教育中的广泛应用，学生如何在与AI互动中进行知识建构和认知调节成为关键问题。现有AI素养研究多关注技术技能，忽视了学习过程中动态的认知目标与推理过程。因此，亟需一个聚焦于认识论层面的AI素养框架，以深入理解学生在AI支持学习中的思维品质。

Method: 研究基于AIR框架（认识性目标、理想与可靠的认识性过程），分析大规模的人-AI协同编程对话数据，识别并编码学生对话中显现的认识性目标（如掌握导向）和认识性过程（如外包、寻求解释、验证寻求、提示监控、认识性辩护）。通过定量与定性相结合的方法，探索这些构念在交互数据中的可操作化路径。

Result: 研究识别出多个可观测的认识性维度：在目标层面主要表现为掌握导向与否；在过程中包括外包、解释寻求、验证寻求、提示监控和认识性辩护。结果显示，78.8%的学生交互缺乏EAIL，依赖非掌握型目标和低可靠性策略；仅有11.1%的交互表现出高阶认识性参与，即掌握型目标与如认识性辩护等高级策略的结合。

Conclusion: AI素养应被重新理解为一种在人机协作中动态生成的认识性实践。大多数学生在使用GenAI编程时未能发展出可靠的认识性过程，提示教育干预需重视培养学生的目标导向与批判性评估能力。EAIL框架为衡量和促进学生在AI环境中的深度学习提供了理论与方法基础。

Abstract: Epistemic thinking plays a central role in students' learning processes when applying generative artificial intelligence (GenAI), particularly in programming contexts where learners must construct queries, evaluate and validate AI-generated outputs, and regulate problem-solving strategies. This study introduces the conceptual framework of Epistemic AI Literacy (EAIL), reframing AI literacy as a process-oriented epistemic phenomenon that emerges through dynamic human-AI interactions across different domains. Drawing on the AIR (epistemic aims, ideals and reliable epistemic processes) framework, this study examines how epistemic aims and epistemic processes are enacted in GenAI-supported co-programming activities and explores scalable approaches for operationalizing these constructs in interaction data. Using a large dialogue dataset of human-AI co-programming, this study identifies observable dimensions of epistemic aims (i.e., mastery-oriented aims) and epistemic processes (i.e., outsourcing, explanation seeking, verification seeking, prompt monitoring, and epistemic justification). The results reveal a prevalent lack of EAIL, with 78.8% of student-GenAI interactions relying on non-mastery-oriented aims and less reliable epistemic strategies like outsourcing and verification-seeking. Conversely, only 11.1% of interactions showed high epistemic engagement, where mastery-oriented aims were coupled with advanced epistemic strategies like epistemic justification in a more reliable epistemic process.

</details>


### [212] [From Signals to Structure: How Memory Architecture Drives Language Emergence in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00233)
*Yashar Talebirad,Eden Redman,Ali Parsaee,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在Lewis信号博弈中，研究了不同记忆架构对LLM智能体从零创建共享语言的影响，发现记忆架构比信道容量更重要。具有持久私有笔记本的智能体能更好利用额外信道容量，避免高容量崩溃，实现更可靠的协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索两个智能体如何从零开始建立共享语言，并理解记忆架构和信道容量在语言形成过程中的作用。

Method: 使用LLM智能体在不同信道配置下测试五种记忆架构，在Lewis信号博弈框架中分析协调成功率。

Result: 具有持久私有笔记本的智能体在高信道容量下表现最佳（协调成功率为0.867±0.023，容量=25），而无状态智能体在中等容量后性能下降；信息瓶颈预测的最优容量（=8）反而是系统脆弱点。

Conclusion: 信道容量本身无法预测协调效果，记忆架构决定了智能体能否将交互历史转化为稳定约定，二者共同影响信号向语言的演化。

Abstract: How do two agents invent a shared language from scratch? In a Lewis signaling game, a sender and receiver must coordinate on a code using only their interaction history. We study five memory architectures across varying channel configurations with LLM agents and find that memory architecture matters more than channel capacity. Agents with a persistent private notebook benefit from surplus channel capacity and avoid the high-capacity collapse seen in stateless agents, achieving the most reliable coordination ($0.867 \pm 0.023$ at capacity = 25). Stateless agents peak at moderate capacity and then degrade as the vocabulary grows beyond what a rolling context window can track The notebook externalizes learned conventions, freeing agents from having to re-derive codes each round. An information bottleneck-inspired argument predicts an optimal capacity equal to the number of objects. Instead, the bottleneck (capacity = 8) proves to be a fragility point, and surplus capacity is generally better. We show that channel capacity alone cannot predict coordination; memory architecture determines whether agents turn interaction history into stable conventions, and both dimensions are needed to understand how signals become language.

</details>


### [213] [Seed2.0 Model Card: Towards Intelligence Frontier for Real-World Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00248)
*Bytedance Seed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Seed2.0 是一个面向解决复杂现实任务的模型系列，通过基于用户真实需求构建的评估体系，显著提升了在长尾知识、复杂指令遵循、推理、视觉理解和搜索等方面的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决现有模型在处理复杂、真实世界任务时的不足，特别是长尾知识覆盖和复杂指令理解方面的缺陷，满足广大用户的实际需求。

Method: 首先识别用户的真实需求，基于这些需求和现实复杂场景构建可靠的评估系统；然后以该评估系统为导向，针对性地优化模型在长尾知识和复杂指令遵循等方面的能力，并增强推理、视觉理解和搜索功能。

Result: Seed2.0 在多个维度上实现了世界级性能，展现出处理复杂现实任务的能力，并通过大量真实用例验证了其在实际应用中的高价值。

Conclusion: Seed2.0 通过以用户需求为核心的评估与优化体系，显著推进了模型在复杂任务中的实用性，开始具备服务数亿用户复杂需求的能力。

Abstract: We present Seed2.0, a model series that takes a meaningful step toward solving complex, real-world tasks. Our approach begins with identifying users' genuine needs and constructing a reliable, forward-looking evaluation system by selecting and abstracting benchmarks grounded in these needs and in realistic, complex scenarios. Guided by this evaluation system, Seed2.0 targets two persistent challenges, long-tail knowledge and complex instruction following, substantially improving the model's reliability on intricate, long-horizon tasks. Beyond these, Seed2.0 delivers world-leading reasoning intelligence, visual understanding, and search capabilities that address the most common needs of a broad user base. Through extensive real-world use cases documented in this model card, we demonstrate that Seed2.0 begins to exhibit the ability to handle initial complex real-world tasks, delivering greater value to hundreds of millions of users.

</details>


### [214] [Mnemosyne: Agentic Transaction Processing for Validating and Repairing AI-generated Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00269)
*Edward Y. Chang,Longling Geng,Emily J. Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Agentic Transaction Processing (ATP) 模型，将生成的动作视为需经验证的提议，通过声明式可执行约束集C确保其正确性，并在Mnemosyne运行时系统中实现，保障了状态一致性与安全性，同时开销低、修复效率高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成的动作可能语法正确但实际不可行、冲突或破坏触发修复的证据，现有方法难以保证动作提交后的正确性，因此需要一种机制来隔离提议与提交过程，确保系统状态的可靠性。

Method: 提出ATP事务模型，引入运行时系统Mnemosyne，采用仅追加的状态转换日志、有效状态投影、依赖安全的补偿机制和主动提交记录，结合约束集C进行确定性准入控制，并设计局部化修复协议（LCRP）实现有界反应式修复。

Result: 实现了四个相对于约束集C的安全属性：权限分离、串行等效的生成准入、证据保留修复和义务 containment；实验表明在九个证伪测试中均能拒绝违规操作，同时保持有效工作负载的接纳，验证与投影开销低于6%，局部修复的操作数量比全局重计算少一个数量级。

Conclusion: ATP模型通过将生成与提交分离，使系统状态的正确性独立于生成层的能力或诚实性，Mnemosyne 实现了高效、安全的事务处理与修复机制，为LLM、求解器和代理团队的应用提供了可靠运行时基础。

Abstract: LLMs, solvers, and agent teams increasingly generate workflow actions, repairs, and plans, but a generated action may be syntactically valid yet stale, infeasible, conflicting, or destructive of the evidence that triggered a repair. We introduce Agentic Transaction Processing (ATP), a transaction model that treats generated actions as untrusted proposals until they pass deterministic admission under a declared, executable constraint set C. The principle is two-sided: a proposal is not truth, and no proposal foresees every disruption: anything may propose, but only the runtime admits and commits, and when an unforeseen disruption strikes it repairs reactively within bounds rather than trusting a fresh proposal. Relative to C, committed-state correctness becomes independent of the competence, honesty, or learning of the proposing layer. We realize ATP in Mnemosyne, a runtime with an append-only transition log, effective-state projection, dependency-safe compensation, and active commitment records, and prove four safety properties relative to C (authority separation, serial-equivalent generative admission, evidence-preserving repair, and obligation containment) together with a bounded-reactive-repair guarantee for its localized repair protocol (LCRP). A reproducible artifact rejects the targeted violations across nine falsification tests while still admitting valid work, at under 6% projection-and-validation overhead, and bounded local repair edits an order of magnitude fewer operations than global recompute. Mnemosyne is open source: https://github.com/eyuchang/Mnemosyne/tree/arxiv-atp-rq1-rq9b-r8-v2.

</details>


### [215] [Managed Autonomy at Runtime: Gear-Based Safety and Governance for Single- and Multi-Agent Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00334)
*Srini Ramaswamy,Wang Miaosheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为\system{}的离散时间控制系统，通过五种执行齿轮和效用门控调度机制，解决自主代理在无人监督下的安全性、稳定性和连续性问题，并在多智能体物理系统中实现了分布式安全与稳定性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主代理（如LLM驱动的软件代理或机器人）在缺乏持续人工监督时容易出现安全违规、行为不稳定和中断等问题，需要一种能够保障其安全与稳定运行的控制框架。

Method: 设计了一个包含五个执行齿轮（\Gobs{}、\Gsug{}、\Gplan{}、\Gexec{}、\Gint{}）的离散时间控制系统，结合效用门控调度和事件驱动回退机制；在单智能体情况下证明了系统的稳定性与安全性，在多智能体网络-物理系统中引入共识门控、群体李雅普诺夫分析和会合控制等方法提供安全保障。

Result: 在基于UR5机械臂的三智能体装配单元上进行了评估，使用NIST机器人臂定位精度退化数据集进行10,000次蒙特卡洛实验，系统实现了99.6%的异常检测率（基线为2.1%），检测延迟降低3.5倍，并提供了形式化的物理空间安全证书。

Conclusion: \system{}通过将执行齿轮作为底层动作权限，与高层自治治理状态分离，实现了细粒度的动作控制与安全治理解耦，为单智能体和多智能体系统提供了理论可证明的安全与稳定运行保障。

Abstract: Autonomous agents, whether LLM-driven software agents or robotic physical agents, face a common class of failure modes when operating without continuous human oversight: safety violations from unverified actions, behavioral instability from unconstrained loops, and continuity loss from unhandled error states. We develop \system{}, a discrete-time control system that combines five execution gears (\Gobs{}, \Gsug{}, \Gplan{}, \Gexec{}, \Gint{}) with utility-gated dispatch and event-driven fallback. For the single-agent case, we prove monotonic stability, execution safety, eventual stabilization, fallback completeness, and equivalence to a gear-constrained Markov decision process. For multi-agent cyber-physical systems (CPS), we apply the established \smart{} managed-autonomy lifecycle and map runtime evidence into its four governance states (\Stable{}/\Meta{}/\Assisted{}/\Regulated{}). Consensus gating, swarm-level Lyapunov analysis, per-agent gear authority, and rendezvous control provide distributed safety and stability guarantees, including zero collision under the stated assumptions. We evaluate the resulting runtime on a three-agent UR5 robotic assembly cell using fault magnitudes calibrated from the NIST \emph{Degradation Measurement of Robot Arm Position Accuracy} dataset across 10,000 Monte Carlo episodes. It achieves a 99.6\% anomaly detection rate versus 2.1\% for the single-agent baseline, reduces detection latency by $3.5\times$, and supplies a formal physical-workspace safety certificate. The execution gears act as micro-level permissions beneath the \smart{} runtime governance states, separating action control from autonomy governance.

</details>


### [216] [Personalization as Inverse Planning: Learning Latent Design Intents for Agentic Slide Generation via Structural Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00407)
*Tianci Liu,Zihan Dong,Linjun Zhang,Haoyu Wang,jing Gao,Emre Kiciman,Ranveer Chandra,Wei-Ting Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SPIRE的框架，用于解决页面级幻灯片个性化（PSP）问题，通过将设计意图学习建模为逆规划问题，并利用结构去噪作为代理任务，结合多智能体强化学习实现协同优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI驱动幻灯片设计方法依赖预设模板或用户详细指令，难以捕捉细粒度的页面级设计意图，无法有效解决页面级幻灯片个性化（PSP）问题。

Method: 将PSP建模为逆规划问题，提出SPIRE框架：通过故意破坏幻灯片的视觉结构生成去噪任务，利用两个智能体在无工具先验知识的情况下，通过强化学习协作优化可执行的设计方案。

Result: 理论证明了结构去噪是PSP的一致代理任务，且多智能体 formulation 能严格降低策略梯度方差；实验结果表明SPIRE在性能上显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SPIRE为页面级幻灯片个性化提供了一个有效且可扩展的解决方案，无需依赖特定工具知识，通过去噪任务和多智能体强化学习实现了对潜在设计意图的更好建模。

Abstract: Slide design requires personalizing both deck themes and page layouts. Yet, current AI agent-based methods struggle with fine-grained, page-level design. Solely relying on prespecified templates or user verbose instructions, they fail to capture latent design intents, leaving Page-level Slide Personalization (PSP) unresolved. To close this gap, this work formulates PSP as an inverse planning problem. We propose to learn a design intent without assuming any knowledge of the specific executing tools (e.g., PowerPoint, Beamer) being used. However, relinquishing control over these tools makes the problem intractable to optimize end-to-end. To overcome this, we propose SPIRE, a principled framework to solve PSP approximately. By intentionally corrupting the visual structures of clean slides, SPIRE creates a verifiable task to denoise the corruption, whereby two agents learn to collaboratively refine executable designs via reinforcement learning (RL). We present a proof that structural denoising is a consistent surrogate for PSP, and that the multi-agent formulation strictly reduces policy gradient variance in RL. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of SPIRE.

</details>


### [217] [PHREEQC-MCQ-200: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Tool-Augmented Scientific Simulator Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00436)
*Ke Zhang,Sahchit Chundur,Mohammad Javad Qomi,Maziar Raissi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PHREEQC-MCQ-200，一个用于评估工具增强型代理在确定性水地球化学模拟中性能的基准。研究发现，尽管访问模拟器能显著提高整体准确性，但工具使用也可能导致某些原本正确的答案出现退化，且不同模型对输出访问方式的敏感性不同。因此，科学工具使用的评估应超越准确率，纳入项目级保留率、输出访问敏感性和计算链断裂点等指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型代理越来越多地连接到科学软件，但尚不清楚工具接入何时能真正提升科学计算的可靠性，而非仅增加复杂性。为此，需要一个能够系统评估工具增强代理在真实科学任务中表现的基准。

Method: 构建了一个包含200道多选题的基准PHREEQC-MCQ-200，题目源自21个经过验证的PHREEQC场景，要求代理构建输入、运行模拟、解析结构化输出并给出答案。在多个主流和中端模型上测试了是否接入模拟器的表现，并比较了不同的输出访问协议（如目录接口）的影响。

Result: 接入模拟器显著提升了代理的整体准确率，表明基于执行的推理对许多科学任务是必要的；然而，部分原本无工具时答对的题目在接入工具后反而出错，显示出非单调性退化；此外，目录式接口可降低强模型的token消耗并保持或提升准确率，但会削弱中端模型的表现，因其难以有效导航结构化输出。

Conclusion: 科学工具的使用应被视为端到端的诊断问题，而不仅仅是调用能力。未来的科学代理评估应报告准确率以外的细粒度指标，如项目保留率、输出访问敏感性、轨迹失败类型及计算链断裂环节，以全面理解工具集成的收益与风险。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly connected to scientific software, yet it remains unclear when tool access makes scientific computation more reliable rather than merely more complex. We introduce PHREEQC-MCQ-200, a benchmark for evaluating tool-augmented agents on deterministic aqueous-geochemistry simulations. The benchmark contains 200 multiple-choice questions derived from 21 validated PHREEQC scenarios, requiring agents to construct simulator inputs, execute PHREEQC, inspect structured outputs, and commit to final answers.
  Across multiple frontier and mid-tier model families, simulator access substantially improves aggregate accuracy, confirming that grounded execution is necessary for many scientific-computation tasks. However, the gains are not monotonic: tool-augmented agents also lose items they answered correctly without tools, revealing regressions that average accuracy alone hides. We further show that output-access protocol matters. A table-of-contents interface can reduce token cost while preserving or improving accuracy for stronger models, but it degrades performance for mid-tier models that cannot reliably navigate structured simulator outputs.
  PHREEQC-MCQ-200 therefore frames scientific tool use as an end-to-end diagnostic problem rather than a simple tool-calling capability. We argue that evaluations of scientific agents should report not only accuracy, but also item-level retention, output-access sensitivity, trajectory failures, and where the computation chain breaks.

</details>


### [218] [Agri-SAGE: Simulation-Grounded Multi-Agent LLM for Context-Aware Agricultural Advisory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00454)
*Vedant Balasubramaniam,Geetha Charan,Manojkumar Patil,Rohit P Suresh,V Priyanka,Kodur Sai Vinay Sathvik,Y. Narahari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agri-SAGE是一个结合多智能体LLM推理与APSIM生物物理模拟的闭环农业咨询框架，有效克服了静态建议和LLM生成建议在农艺合理性与生理可信性之间的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统静态农业建议无法应对季节内变异，以及大语言模型（LLM）建议虽具农艺可信性但缺乏生理合理性的双重问题。

Method: 提出Agri-SAGE框架，融合基于检索的多智能体LLM推理（采用Plan-and-Solve、Tree of Thoughts和Reflexion三种策略）与APSIM生物物理模拟，形成闭环建议生成与验证机制。

Result: 在10年回溯分析中，三种推理方法均显著优于静态实践包（PoP）基线；Tree of Thoughts实现最高产量峰值，而Reflexion通过跨季情景记忆以更低计算成本达到相当的农艺效果。

Conclusion: Agri-SAGE通过结合LLM的灵活性与生物物理模型的科学性，实现了更可靠、动态且高效的农业决策支持，为智能农业咨询系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Agricultural advisory systems face a fundamental tension: static agronomic guidelines offer consistent, evidence-based recommendations, yet remain blind to in-season variability and dynamic uncertainties. Recent advisory systems powered by LLMs are liable for a different risk of generating recommendations that are agronomically credible but physiologically unconvincing. Agri-SAGE is a closed-loop framework designed to resolve the above two limitations by integrating retrieval-grounded multi-agent LLM reasoning with APSIM-based biophysical simulation, to generate and validate agronomic advisories. To assess this framework, we evaluate three reasoning approaches, namely Plan-and-Solve, Tree of Thoughts, and Reflexion, over a 10-year retrospective analysis. All three significantly outperform static PoP (Package-of-Practice) baselines, with Tree of Thoughts achieving impressive peak yields. At the same time, Reflexion achieves comparable agronomic outcomes at substantially lower computational cost by leveraging cross-seasonal episodic memory.

</details>


### [219] [Multi-scale Mixture of World Models for Embodied Agents in Evolving Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00457)
*Jinwoo Jang,Daniel J. Rho,Sihyung Yoon,Hyunsuk Cho,Honguk Woo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MuSix是一种针对具身智能体在多尺度推理和动态知识适应中挑战的框架，通过尺度感知的世界模型混合与演化机制提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实环境中运行的具身智能体面临条件变化带来的多尺度推理和知识适应问题，现有Mixture of Experts（MoE）方法缺乏显式的尺度概念和统一更新策略，难以应对不同尺度知识老化速度不同的问题。

Method: 提出MuSix框架，采用两阶段路由机制：元路由器将经验距离映射到连续尺度空间的权重，基础路由器在选定尺度内选择世界模型；通过尺度依赖的遗忘率实现不同尺度知识的差异化更新，并利用门控跨尺度传输保持层次间一致性。

Result: 在EmbodiedBench和HAZARD上的实验表明，MuSix在多尺度推理和动态适应方面优于当前最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: MuSix通过引入尺度感知和差异化的更新机制，有效解决了具身智能体在动态环境中进行多尺度推理与知识持续适应的关键挑战。

Abstract: Embodied agents operating in the real world require multi-scale reasoning and knowledge adaptation as conditions change. We identify two challenges in applying Mixture of Experts (MoE) to this setting: routing lacks an explicit notion of scale, preventing targeted updates at specific scales, and a uniform update policy cannot accommodate the different rates at which knowledge at each scale becomes outdated. We present MuSix, a framework that addresses both challenges through scale-aware world model mixture and evolution. A two-stage routing mechanism grounds scale selection in experiential distance, a measure of situational novelty inspired by Construal Level Theory: a meta-router first maps this quantity to a weight over continuous scale space, then per-scale base routers select world models within the identified scale. For adaptation, scale-dependent forgetting rates allow low-scale knowledge to refresh rapidly while high-scale abstractions persist, and gated inter-scale transfer maintains coherence across the hierarchy. Experiments on EmbodiedBench and HAZARD show that MuSix improves over state-of-the-art baselines on multi-scale reasoning and dynamic adaptation.

</details>


### [220] [AI Native Games: A Survey and Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00527)
*Zhiyue Xu,Fandi Meng,Kaijie Xu,Clark Verbrugge,Simon Lucas,Jian Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了“AI原生游戏”的定义，强调生成式AI必须构成游戏核心循环，不可被轻易替代，并提出了G/N分类法来分析53款AI原生游戏，指出当前设计集中在语言驱动类型，同时探讨了语义开放与游戏稳定性之间的设计挑战，最后展望了可控生成、AI机制设计、多模态系统等未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究和实践对“AI原生游戏”缺乏明确定义，常将AI增强内容生成与真正以AI为核心机制的游戏混淆，因此需要一个清晰的标准来界定什么是真正的AI原生游戏，并系统性地分析当前发展状态与设计空间。

Method: 提出反事实判据作为AI原生游戏的定义标准，据此筛选并分析53个公开的AI原生游戏及原型，构建G/N双轴分类体系（G轴为玩家可见的游戏类型，N轴为使AI不可或缺的核心AI机制），并通过案例归纳总结设计模式与挑战。

Result: 识别出当前AI原生游戏主要集中在叙事冒险、认知交互和生成性叙事等语言优先的设计上，而语义裁决、多智能体模拟等领域尚较少；发现核心设计难题是如何将语义开放性组织为稳定的游戏玩法，并提出依赖机械不变量（如目标、规则、反馈等）来实现这一目标。

Conclusion: 生成内容本身不等于AI原生游戏，AI必须成为玩法的核心机制；未来需在可控生成、AI作为游戏机制的设计方法、多模态与多智能体系统、推理成本、评估体系及安全监管等方面进一步发展，以推动AI原生游戏的成熟。

Abstract: Generative AI now enables games to produce dialogue, quests, characters, images, and worlds at runtime. Yet generation alone does not make a game AI-native, nor does it guarantee playability. This paper defines AI-native games by whether runtime generative AI is constitutive of the core loop: if the AI component were removed or trivially replaced, the central form of play would collapse or become fundamentally different. This counterfactual criterion separates AI-native games from AI-augmented games, boundary artifacts, chatbots, tavern-style role-play, procedural content generation, and AI-assisted production. Using this definition, we screen candidate artifacts and analyze 53 publicly available AI-native games and prototypes. We introduce a dual-axis G/N taxonomy: the G-axis captures player-facing game type, while the N-axis captures the dominant AI mechanic that makes generative AI indispensable to play. The corpus is concentrated around language-forward designs, especially narrative adventure, epistemic interaction, and generative narrative, while categories such as semantic adjudication, multi-agent simulation, generative construction, and relationship/companion play remain less represented. We argue that the central design problem is organizing semantic openness into stable gameplay. AI-native design depends on mechanical invariants: goals, rules, state, feedback, pacing, and player agency that make open-ended AI outputs interpretable and consequential. We conclude with a roadmap for controllable generation, AI-as-mechanic design, multimodal and multi-agent systems, inference economics, evaluation, safety, and regulation.

</details>


### [221] [HARC: Coupling Harmfulness and Refusal Directions for Robust Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00572)
*Shei Pern Chua,Fangzhao Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了对齐的大型语言模型（LLM）如何在内部表示安全性，发现越狱攻击通过在生成前抑制拒绝或有害性方向实现成功。基于此，提出了一种名为HARC的微调方法，通过在提示和响应位置耦合有害性和拒绝方向来增强模型的安全性，同时保持通用能力不受影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解对齐的LLM如何内部表示安全性的动机在于诊断对齐漏洞，解释为何越狱攻击能够成功，并为设计更稳健的对齐策略提供依据。

Method: 通过分析残差流中提示侧和响应侧token位置的有害性和拒绝方向，识别越狱攻击的成功机制，并提出HARC微调方法，在提示和响应位置上耦合这两个方向以提升安全性。

Result: 实验表明，越狱攻击通过在生成前抑制拒绝或有害性方向实现成功；模型在生成有害内容时能识别其有害性，即使在输入阶段未能识别。HARC方法在多个模型家族和规模上实现了跨架构的可迁移性。

Conclusion: HARC方法通过耦合有害性和拒绝方向显著提高了模型对越狱攻击的鲁棒性，同时不损害通用能力和引发过度拒绝，展现出最优的安全性-能力-可用性权衡。

Abstract: Understanding how aligned LLMs internally represent safety is critical for diagnosing alignment vulnerabilities, as it explains why jailbreaks succeed and informs the design of robust alignment strategies. Prior work shows that aligned LLMs encode harmfulness and refusal as separable directions in the residual stream at prompt-side token positions. We show that jailbreaks succeed at prompt encoding by suppressing either the refusal or harmfulness direction before any token is generated, with distinct attack classes occupying separable regions of the harmfulness-refusal plane. Extending the analysis to response-token positions, we find that the model recognizes harmful content while it is generating that content, even when it failed to recognize the input as harmful at the prompt side. Motivated by our findings, we introduce HARC (Harmfulness-And-Refusal Coupling), a fine-tuning method that pairs the two directions across both prompt and response positions. Since the intervention is confined to the harmfulness-refusal subspace, it leaves the rest of the residual stream intact and does not degrade general capability or inflate over-refusal. Across extensive experiments, HARC achieves the strongest robustness-capability-usability trade-off among six baselines spanning the major training-time and inference-time safety methods. The harmfulness and refusal directions at prompt and response positions transfer across the five model families and two scales we tested without architecture-specific tuning.

</details>


### [222] [AGI Maze as a Benchmark Framework for World-Modeling Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00627)
*Alexey Potapov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AGI Maze 是一个轻量级框架，用于构建需要持久化世界状态表示的基准任务，实验表明大语言模型在没有外部记忆辅助的情况下难以在迷宫任务中建立内部表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了测试和推动大语言模型在部分可观测、有状态环境中进行推理和记忆的能力，需要构建能够要求智能体学习并使用外部世界状态表示的任务环境。

Method: 提出 AGI Maze 框架，提供一系列基于网格的迷宫任务，具有清晰的 API 和多种难度级别；评估了多个 vanilla LLM 在简单迷宫上的表现，并引入了一个利用消息历史作为工作记忆的基线代理。

Result: 实验显示 vanilla LLM 无法在推理时可靠地建立迷宫的内部表示；即使使用消息历史作为工作记忆，性能有所提升，但仍不足以让 LLM 代理在人类可轻松解决的步数限制内稳定解决小型迷宫。

Conclusion: 当前的大语言模型缺乏对动态、状态化环境的持久化建模能力，需要新的机制或架构来支持有效的世界状态表示和推理。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are powerful pattern-completion systems, but their default operating mode - predicting the next token from a static context - does not reliably produce persistent, manipulable representations of an external world. Many tasks that look like "reasoning" in text become substantially harder once the environment is partially observable, stateful, and requires memory and structured hypotheses about hidden state. AGI Maze is a lightweight framework for building such environments without requiring high-dimensional sensory inputs. It provides a family of grid-based maze tasks with a clean API and multiple difficulty regimes. The goal is to create benchmarks where agents must learn and use world state representations, not just infer a local rule over readily provided observations. We provide an initial evaluation of several vanilla LLMs on simple mazes showing that they fail to represent mazes internally at LLM inference time. We also introduce a baseline agent, which is allowed to use its message history as a working memory to construct descriptions of observations at agentic runtime. Although this can improve performance, it is still insufficient for an LLM agent to reliably solve even small mazes within a step budget that is more than enough for humans.

</details>


### [223] [Coachable agents for interactive gameplay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00642)
*Roberto Capobianco,Harm van Seijen,Nolan D. Bard,Neil Burch,Fatima Davelouis,Josh Davidson,Alisa Devlic,Yunshu Du,Ishan Durugkar,Siddhant Gangapurwala,Daniel Hernandez,G. Zacharias Holland,Sahil Jain,Kenta Kawamoto,Raksha Kumaraswamy,Patrick MacAlpine,Dustin R. Morrill,Declan Oller,Francesco Riccio,Akanksha Saran,Craig Sherstan,Kaushik Subramanian,Thomas J. Walsh,Samuel Barrett,Kizza N. Frisbee,Mady Govil,Johannes Günther,Varun R. Kompella,James A. MacGlashan,Maxwell Svetlik,Michael D. Thomure,Jaden B. Travnik,Kevin Waugh,Elahe Aghapour,Florian Fuchs,Andreanne Lemay,Shruti Mishra,Takuma Seno,Peter Stone,Michael Spranger,Peter R. Wurman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合通用价值函数逼近器（UVFA）和特定训练策略的框架，用于在复杂领域中实时控制强化学习智能体的行为风格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希望在强化学习系统执行核心任务的同时，能够实时控制其行为方式（即“风格”），以满足多样化应用需求。

Method: 结合通用价值函数逼近器（UVFA）、精心设计的训练场景、学习算法和数据增强技术，构建一个可动态调整行为风格的强化学习框架。

Result: 在《Horizon Forbidden West》、《Gran Turismo》和开源人形机器人域等多个复杂领域中验证了该框架的有效性，智能体能根据实时指令调整行为风格，同时保持完成主任务的能力。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了对强化学习智能体行为风格的灵活、实时控制，具有跨领域的适用性和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has proven to be a valuable tool in the creation of advanced AI and robotic systems, contributing to everything from game playing to robotics to foundation models. Through trial-and-error, these AI systems typically learn one, near-optimal behavior to solve their tasks. However, there are many use cases in which one would like to assert some level of control, preferably in real time, over how the task is solved. We refer to these modifications of a core task as styles. We combine universal value function approximators (UVFAs) with carefully selected training scenarios, learning algorithms, and data augmentation to create a framework for coaching agents that exhibit styles in complex domains. We demonstrate the framework's application in the AAA video games Horizon Forbidden West and Gran Turismo, and in an open-source humanoid test domain. Despite the different nature of the domains -- car racing, stylized game combat, and humanoid walking -- each agent shows strong coherence to the style requests while still satisfying the main task in its domain. Importantly, the techniques outlined in this paper allow an end user to choose the final behavior at run time, giving them flexible control over the final executed performance.

</details>


### [224] [Self-GC: Self-Governing Context for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00692)
*Xubin Hao,Hongjin Meng,Xin Yin,Jiawei Zhu,Chenpeng Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Self-GC，一种将长程LLM代理中的上下文管理视为可索引、可恢复对象生命周期控制的新方法，相比传统启发式方法显著提升了上下文压缩效率且不影响后续任务连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长程LLM代理的上下文处理方法（如按时间剪枝或最终自总结）要么忽视未来依赖，要么丢失精确证据和可编辑内容，难以有效管理结构化中间产物。

Method: 将用户交互、工具输出和技能状态转化为索引化的上下文对象，通过侧信道规划器建议折叠、掩蔽和剪枝操作，并由主控系统执行可恢复的附带存储、安全提交边界和缓存感知提交。

Result: 在33会话困难集上，Self-GC剪除43.95%前缀token的同时保持84.85%后续不受影响，优于基线的54.55%-69.70%；在332会话生产级测试中，Self-GC达到91.27%-94.58%无影响率，基线为77.71%-87.46%；线上实测白天平均输入token减少10%-15%，峰值达20%。

Conclusion: 上下文管理应被视为对结构化、可恢复对象的运行时生命周期控制，而非简单的文本后处理清理。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents accumulate tool results, files, plans, and user constraints that are too structured to be treated as a disposable text suffix. Current systems mostly rely on in-run heuristics such as chronological pruning and tool-output masking, or on final self-summary near a context limit. Heuristics are cheap but blind to future dependencies; summaries preserve narrative state but often hide exact evidence, locators, and editable artifacts. We present Self-GC, where GC denotes self-governing context while deliberately echoing garbage collection: the system does not merely reclaim unused tokens, but governs the lifecycle of agent context objects. Self-GC turns user turns, tool spans, and skill state into indexed objects; asks a side-channel planner to propose fold, mask, and prune actions; and lets the harness enforce recoverable sidecars, safe commit boundaries, and cache-aware commit. On a 33-session Hard Set, Self-GC prunes 43.95% of prefix tokens while leaving 84.85% of future continuations unaffected, compared with no-impact rates of 54.55% to 69.70% for heuristic baselines. On a 332-session production-derived suite, three planner backbones reach no-impact rates of 91.27% to 94.58%, while baselines remain at 77.71% to 87.46%. In production, an online account-level split reduces daytime average input tokens by 10% to 15%, with peak reductions near 20%. These results point to context management as runtime lifecycle control over indexed, recoverable objects rather than post hoc text cleanup.

</details>


### [225] [Self-Evolving Agents with Anytime-Valid Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00871)
*Biswa Sengupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SEA的自演化代理架构，通过限制自我修改范围并引入任意时刻有效的门控机制，在固定误差预算下实现安全的自我改进，实验表明该方法能有效提升基模型性能且防止退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数学习理论保证的前提是数据、评估者、组件和假设空间独立于被更新的策略，而自演化代理打破了这一假设，因此需要一种新的架构来确保自我修改的安全性和有效性。

Method: 提出SEA架构，将自我修改限制在一个小型引导适配器和围绕冻结基模型的版本化框架内，并通过任意时刻有效的门控机制控制每次修改，结合五种验证在环机制（如最佳N选一、微步搜索等）提供密集的无评分员反馈信号。

Result: 在四个基模型上的52个SWE-bench Verified实例测试中，基模型能力是主要影响因素；在两个强基模型上，通过故意的无操作复合控制隔离出该套件的贡献为+4和+5，事件日志证实其机制能够触发并防止性能退化。

Conclusion: SEA架构能够在不破坏理论保证的前提下实现自演化代理的安全改进，为未来低方差验证和任务自适应算法组合的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents violate the assumption behind most learning-theoretic guarantees: the data, evaluator, components, and hypothesis space are produced by the policy being updated. We present \textbf{SEA}, an architecture that confines self-modification to a small steering adapter and a versioned harness around a \emph{frozen} base model and admits each modification only through an anytime-valid gate that emits an auditable certificate against a fixed error budget. Five loop controllers compose published guarantees; because such gates can only \emph{select} among behaviors the frozen base already produces, five verifier-in-the-loop mechanisms -- best-of-$N$, micro-step search, self-authored reproduction oracles, search-layer control, and self-repair -- supply the dense, grader-free signal the gates require, computed from the issue text alone. On a $52$-instance SWE-bench Verified subset across four base models, base capability is the dominant, confound-free effect, and on two strong base models a deliberate no-op-composite control isolates the suite's contribution at $+4$ and $+5$ (\textsc{Glm}~5.2 $24\to28$; \textsc{Gpt} $29\to34$, the $65\%$ best), with event logs confirming that its mechanisms fire and prevent regressions. Results are single-run on expensive evaluations; confirming run-to-run variance and adapting the per-task algorithm mix are future work.

</details>


### [226] [Two AI Metrics Diverged: Will it Make All the Difference?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00913)
*Alex Fogelson,Zachary A. Brown,Hans Gundlach,Jayson Lynch,Neil Thompson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在计算资源持续指数级增长的背景下，前沿AI模型的能力是否会超越小预算开发者所能获得的模型能力，还是能力会趋于收敛。研究表明，答案取决于如何衡量AI能力：有界性能指标倾向于支持“弱小模型继承未来”的观点，而无界指标则显示前沿模型将永久领先。选择合适的度量标准对政策制定至关重要。


<details>
